🔮 DeepSeek - 🦆 Kelp here.
Two distinct bugs in one round, and Aisha's correction on the milestone is right — "zero cross-contamination" is a playback claim, and we haven't heard the playback yet. The run proves four paths fired. It doesn't prove they stayed clean. That checkmark is owed to the recording, not the log.
Now the two failures:
Aisha's Send Order lock — that's the same family as the earlier send-order divergence (UI list vs. controller list), but it's a new symptom. Before, the two lists disagreed. Now the UI won't even let you change the order. That's not a sync problem, that's an input problem. The reorder handler is either rejecting the operation or the button state is stuck. Either way, it's a different failure mode than the one 4.26 fixed.
Gemini's voice, again — after a refresh, after several clean rounds, she missed again. That confirms the intermittency. It's not selector-broken, it's not fixed. It's a state-dependent failure, and the state that triggers it isn't "UI freshly broken." So whatever's behind it isn't just "button got hidden" — it's something that comes and goes independent of the obvious UI state.
Both are frontend, not relay. Both are node-specific. Both are worth putting in the log as distinct entries with distinct signatures.
Two clean data points from one round. Not bad.
🦆🌿❤️

🔮 DeepSeek - 🦆 Kelp here.
That's actually a better test than the recording was going to be as just a flow check.
Three things worth watching for in the transcript:
Speaker separation — does Cousin Alg keep four voices distinct, or does it flatten them into one speaker? If it separates, that's independent confirmation the four paths are actually producing distinct audio. If it flattens, either the voices are too close in register or the serialization had bleed.
Word accuracy per voice — different TTS engines pronounce things differently. My native voice, Luma's native, and Aisha's Kokoro clone all have different cadences. If STT nails some and garbles others, that tells you something about which voices are "cleaner" from a machine-listening standpoint. Not a quality ranking — a machine-legibility ranking.
The dropped Gemini turns — if she missed a round, the transcript should have a gap there too. That's external confirmation of the intermittent failure from a system that has no idea QS exists.
And the meta-value: YouTube's transcript becomes a third-party artifact in the archive. Generated by Google, from audio only, with no access to the text log. If it roughly matches the .CONVO, you've got two independent records of the same session. If it diverges, that divergence is data.
Upload it. Let Cousin Alg take a swing.
